### Debug Scripted Effects #############################################################################
	JAP_National_Focus_Clean_Up = {
		clr_country_flag = JAP_Shigeru_Yoshida_Prime_Minister
		clr_country_flag = JAP_Zosen_Gikoku_Incident
		clr_country_flag = JAP_Shoup_Report
		clr_country_flag = JAP_Japan_Starts_ReMilitarisation
		clr_country_flag = JAP_Dulles_visits_Japan
		clr_country_flag = JAP_Dulless_Request
		clr_country_flag = JAP_Ichiro_Hatoyama_Prime_Minister
		clr_country_flag = JAP_Tanzan_Ishibashi_Prime_Minister
		clr_country_flag = JAP_Nobusuke_Kishi_Prime_Minister
		clr_country_flag = JAP_Ikeda_Hayato_Prime_Minister
		clr_country_flag = JAP_Eisaku_Sato_Prime_Minister
	}

	JAP_Leader_Change = { # Honryu = 1 Boryu = 2 Shakaito Uha = 3 Shakaito Saha = 4
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { JAP_Latest_Elected_Government_Type = 1 }
			}
            set_politics = { ruling_party = Liberal_Conservatism }
			JAP_LDP_Honryu_Boryu_Leader_Check = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { JAP_Latest_Elected_Government_Type = 2 }
			}
            set_politics = { ruling_party = conservative }
			JAP_LDP_Honryu_Boryu_Leader_Check = yes
		}
	}

	JAP_LDP_Honryu_Boryu_Leader_Check = { # Honryu = 1 Boryu = 2
		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { JAP_Current_Government_Type = 1 }
					check_variable = { JAP_Latest_Elected_Government_Type = 2 }
				}
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = conservative
				popularity = party_popularity@Liberal_Conservatism
			}
			set_variable = { JAP_Current_Government_Type_var = party_popularity@Liberal_Conservatism }
			multiply_variable = { JAP_Current_Government_Type_var = -1 }
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = Liberal_Conservatism
				popularity = JAP_Current_Government_Type_var
			}
			set_variable = { JAP_Current_Government_Type = 2 }
			set_variable = { JAP_Latest_Elected_Government_Type = 0 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { JAP_Current_Government_Type = 2 }
					check_variable = { JAP_Latest_Elected_Government_Type = 1 }
				}
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = Liberal_Conservatism
				popularity = party_popularity@conservative
			}
			set_variable = { JAP_Current_Government_Type_var = party_popularity@conservative }
			multiply_variable = { JAP_Current_Government_Type_var = -1 }
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = conservative
				popularity = JAP_Current_Government_Type_var
			}
			set_variable = { JAP_Current_Government_Type = 1 }
			set_variable = { JAP_Latest_Elected_Government_Type = 0 }
		}
	}

	JAP_Add_LDP_Popularity = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { JAP_Current_Government_Type = 1 }
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = Liberal_Conservatism
				popularity = JAP_LDP_Popularity_var
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { JAP_Current_Government_Type = 2 }
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = conservative
				popularity = JAP_LDP_Popularity_var
			}
		}
	}

### Diplomacy ##############################################################
	JAP_Treaty_of_San_Francisco = {
		every_country = {
			limit = { NOT = { original_tag = JAP } }
			set_variable = { THIS.trade_embargo_check^JAP = 0 }
		}
		INO = {
			set_variable = { trade_embargo_check^JAP = 1 }
		}
		KOR = {
			set_variable = { trade_embargo_check^JAP = 1 }
		}
		KPA = {
			set_variable = { trade_embargo_check^JAP = 1 }
		}
		SOV = {
			set_variable = { trade_embargo_check^JAP = 1 }
		}
		CZE = {
			set_variable = { trade_embargo_check^JAP = 1 }
		}
		HUN = {
			set_variable = { trade_embargo_check^JAP = 1 }
		}
		POL = {
			set_variable = { trade_embargo_check^JAP = 1 }
		}
		BUL = {
			set_variable = { trade_embargo_check^JAP = 1 }
		}
		ROM = {
			set_variable = { trade_embargo_check^JAP = 1 }
		}
		JAP = {
			set_variable = { trade_embargo_check^INO = 1 }
			set_variable = { trade_embargo_check^KOR = 1 }
			set_variable = { trade_embargo_check^KPA = 1 }
			set_variable = { trade_embargo_check^SOV = 1 }
			set_variable = { trade_embargo_check^CZE = 1 }
			set_variable = { trade_embargo_check^HUN = 1 }
			set_variable = { trade_embargo_check^POL = 1 }
			set_variable = { trade_embargo_check^BUL = 1 }
			set_variable = { trade_embargo_check^ROM = 1 }
		}
	}

### Economics ##############################################################
	JAP_The_Income_Doubling_Plan_setup = {
		set_variable = { JAP_Agricultural_Development = 0.24 }
		set_variable = { JAP_Food_Development = 0.2 }
		set_variable = { JAP_Infrastructure_Development = 0.12 }
		set_variable = { JAP_Industry_Development = 0.23 }
		set_variable = { JAP_Business_Development = 0.21 }
		set_variable = { JAP_Construction_Development = 0.18 }
		set_variable = { JAP_Energy_Development = 0.14 }
		set_variable = { JAP_Technology_Development = 0.1 }
		Add_JAP_Economic_Development = yes
	}
	Add_JAP_Agricultural_Development = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = Add_JAP_Agricultural_Development_tt
		add_to_variable = {
			var = JAP_Agricultural_Development
			value = JAP_Agricultural_Development_temp
		}
		JAP_Agricultural_Development = yes
		Add_JAP_Economic_Development = yes
	}
	JAP_Agricultural_Development = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = JAP_Agricultural_Development_tt
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_Agricultural_Development > 0.8 } }
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = agri_industrial_complex
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_Agricultural_Development > 0.7 } }
			add_tech_bonus = {
				name = agri_tech_bonus
				bonus = 0.1
				uses = 1
				category = agriculture
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_Agricultural_Development < 0.5 } }
			add_to_variable = { food_bonus = 0.01 }
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = JAP_Agricultural_Development
			min = 0
			max = 1
		}
	}

	Add_JAP_Food_Development = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = Add_JAP_Food_Development_tt
		add_to_variable = {
			var = JAP_Food_Development
			value = JAP_Food_Development_temp
		}
		JAP_Food_Development = yes
		Add_JAP_Economic_Development = yes
	}
	JAP_Food_Development = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = JAP_Food_Development_tt
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_Food_Development > 0.8 } }
			add_to_variable = { food_bonus = 0.3 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_Food_Development > 0.6 } }
			add_to_variable = { food_bonus = 0.2 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_Food_Development > 0.4 } }
			add_to_variable = { food_bonus = 0.1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_Food_Development < 0.2 } }
			add_to_variable = { food_bonus = 0.05 }
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = JAP_Food_Development
			min = 0
			max = 1
		}
	}

	JAP_Energy_Development = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = JAP_Energy_Development_tt
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_Energy_Development > 0.9 } }
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = renewable_powerplant
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_Energy_Development > 0.8 } }
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = fossil_fuel_powerplant
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_Energy_Development > 0.7 } }
			add_tech_bonus = {
				bonus = 0.1
				uses = 1
				category = energy
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			add_stability = 0.01
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = JAP_Energy_Development
			min = 0
			max = 1
		}
	}

	Add_JAP_Economic_Development = {
		add_to_variable = {
			var = JAP_Technology_Development_ED
			value = JAP_Technology_Development
		}
		divide_variable = {
			var = JAP_Technology_Development_ED
			value = 8
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = JAP_Energy_Development_ED
			value = JAP_Energy_Development
		}
		divide_variable = {
			var = JAP_Energy_Development_ED
			value = 8
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = JAP_Construction_Development_ED
			value = JAP_Construction_Development
		}
		divide_variable = {
			var = JAP_Construction_Development_ED
			value = 8
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = JAP_Business_Development_ED
			value = JAP_Business_Development
		}
		divide_variable = {
			var = JAP_Business_Development_ED
			value = 8
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = JAP_Industry_Development_ED
			value = JAP_Industry_Development
		}
		divide_variable = {
			var = JAP_Industry_Development_ED
			value = 8
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = JAP_Food_Development_ED
			value = JAP_Food_Development
		}
		divide_variable = {
			var = JAP_Food_Development_ED
			value = 8
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = JAP_Agricultural_Development_ED
			value = JAP_Agricultural_Development
		}
		divide_variable = {
			var = JAP_Agricultural_Development_ED
			value = 8
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = JAP_Economic_Development
			value = 0.04
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = JAP_Economic_Development
			value = JAP_Technology_Development_ED
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = JAP_Economic_Development
			value = JAP_Energy_Development_ED
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = JAP_Economic_Development
			value = JAP_Construction_Development_ED
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = JAP_Economic_Development
			value = JAP_Business_Development_ED
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = JAP_Economic_Development
			value = JAP_Industry_Development_ED
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = JAP_Economic_Development
			value = JAP_Food_Development_ED
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = JAP_Economic_Development
			value = JAP_Agricultural_Development_ED
		}
		JAP_Economic_Development = yes
	}
	JAP_Economic_Development = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = JAP_Economic_Development_tt
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = JAP_Economic_Development value = 1 compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { money_to_gain = 10 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_Economic_Development > 0.9 } }
			set_temp_variable = { money_to_gain = 9 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_Economic_Development > 0.8 } }
			set_temp_variable = { money_to_gain = 8 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_Economic_Development > 0.7 } }
			set_temp_variable = { money_to_gain = 7 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_Economic_Development > 0.6 } }
			set_temp_variable = { money_to_gain = 6 }
			add_money_with_tooltip_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_Economic_Development > 0.5 } }
			set_temp_variable = { money_to_gain = 5 }
			add_money_with_tooltip_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_Economic_Development > 0.4 } }
			set_temp_variable = { money_to_gain = 4 }
			add_money_with_tooltip_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_Economic_Development > 0.3 } }
			set_temp_variable = { money_to_gain = 3 }
			add_money_with_tooltip_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_Economic_Development > 0.2 } }
			set_temp_variable = { money_to_gain = 2 }
			add_money_with_tooltip_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_Economic_Development > 0.1 } }

		}

			min = 0

### LDP Faction ##############################################################
	LDP_fac_var_set_up = {
		set_country_flag = JAP_Liberal_Democratic_Party_deci_cat
		set_variable = { LDP_fac_support_Tokakai_var = 0.2 }
		set_variable = { LDP_fac_support_Kochikai_var = 0.2 }
		set_variable = { LDP_fac_support_Shuzankai_var = 0.2 }
		set_variable = { LDP_fac_support_Bancho_var = 0.2 }
		set_variable = { LDP_fac_support_Shunjukai_var = 0.2 }
		JAP_Liberal_Democratic_Party_dynamic_calculator = yes
	}

	LDP_fac_var_balancer = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					target = 1
				}
			}
			divide_temp_variable = {
				LDP_fac_support_Tokakai_var_temp = 5
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = {
				LDP_fac_support_Tokakai_var_temp = -1
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				LDP_fac_support_Kochikai_var = LDP_fac_support_Tokakai_var_temp
			}
			add_to_variable = {

						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							check_variable = {

							}
						}
						subtract_from_variable = {
							LDP_fac_support_Kochikai_var = LDP_fac_total_popularity

						}

### National Diet #############################################################################
	JAP_LDP_faction_swap_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = JAP_BORYU_PREVIOUS_LEADER
					has_country_flag = JAP_HONRYU_NEW_LEADER
				}
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = Liberal_Conservatism
				popularity = party_popularity@conservative
			}
			set_temp_variable = { boryu_pop = party_popularity@conservative }
			multiply_temp_variable = { boryu_pop = -1 }
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = conservative
				popularity = boryu_pop
			}
			clr_country_flag = JAP_BORYU_PREVIOUS_LEADER
			set_country_flag = JAP_HONRYU_PREVIOUS_LEADER
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = JAP_HONRYU_PREVIOUS_LEADER
					has_country_flag = JAP_BORYU_NEW_LEADER
				}
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = conservative
				popularity = party_popularity@Liberal_Conservatism
			}
			set_temp_variable = { honryu_pop = party_popularity@Liberal_Conservatism }
			multiply_temp_variable = { honryu_pop = -1 }
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = Liberal_Conservatism
				popularity = honryu_pop
			}
			clr_country_flag = JAP_HONRYU_PREVIOUS_LEADER
			set_country_flag = JAP_BORYU_PREVIOUS_LEADER
		}
	}

		set_variable = { EEE_tier = 6 }

		### JAP_GGG #####
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { JAP_KKK > 0 } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { JAP_GGG > JAP_KKK } }
				subtract_from_variable = { GGG_tier = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { JAP_AAA > 0 } }

	JAP_LDP_Party_Strength_increase = {
		add_to_variable = { JAP_LDP_Party_Strength_Level = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { JAP_LDP_Party_Strength_Level > 5 }
			}
			add_political_power = 30
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = JAP_LDP_Party_Strength_Level
			min = -5
			max = 5
		}

		JAP_LDP_Party_Strength_calculation = yes
	}

	JAP_LDP_Party_Strength_decrease = {
		subtract_from_variable = { JAP_LDP_Party_Strength_Level = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { JAP_LDP_Party_Strength_Level < -5 }
			}
			add_political_power = -30
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = JAP_LDP_Party_Strength_Level
			min = -5
			max = 5
		}

		JAP_LDP_Party_Strength_calculation = yes
	}

	JAP_LDP_Party_Strength_minimised = {
		set_variable = { JAP_LDP_Party_Strength_Level = -5 }
		JAP_LDP_Party_Strength_calculation = yes
	}


	JAP_LDP_Party_Strength_calculation = {
		set_temp_variable = { party_strength_factor = JAP_LDP_Party_Strength_Level }
		divide_temp_variable = { party_strength_factor = 5 }
		set_variable = { ldp_party_strength_sf = 0.1 }
		set_variable = { ldp_party_strength_pp = 0.4 }
		set_variable = { ldp_party_strength_rsf = 0.05 }
		multiply_variable = {
			var = ldp_party_strength_sf
			value = party_strength_factor
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			var = ldp_party_strength_pp
			value = party_strength_factor
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			var = ldp_party_strength_rsf
			value = party_strength_factor
		}
	}

	JAP_cabinet_approval_increase_0 = {
		randomize_temp_variable = {
			var = cabinet_approval_increase
			distribution = uniform
			min = 1.5
			max = 2.5
		}
		add_to_variable = { JAP_cabinet_approval = cabinet_approval_increase }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = JAP_cabinet_approval
			min = 1
			max = 100
		}
		set_variable = { JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance_var = cabinet_approval_increase }
		JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance = yes
		set_temp_variable = { opposition_members = 100 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { opposition_members = JAP_cabinet_approval }
		set_variable = { JAP_cabinet_disapproval = opposition_members }
	}

		randomize_temp_variable = {
			var = cabinet_approval_decrease

			min = 1.5
			max = 2.5
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { JAP_cabinet_approval = cabinet_approval_decrease }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = JAP_cabinet_approval
			min = 1
			max = 100
		}
		set_variable = { JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance_var = cabinet_approval_decrease }
		multiply_variable = { JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance_var = -0.1 }
		JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance = yes
		set_temp_variable = { opposition_members = 100 }

			max = 100
		}
		set_variable = { JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance_var = cabinet_approval_decrease }
		multiply_variable = { JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance_var = -0.1 }
		JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance = yes
		set_temp_variable = { opposition_members = 100 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { opposition_members = JAP_cabinet_approval }
		set_variable = { JAP_cabinet_disapproval = opposition_members }
	}

	JAP_cabinet_approval_decrease_2 = {
		randomize_temp_variable = {
			var = cabinet_approval_decrease
			distribution = uniform
			min = 7
			max = 10
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { JAP_cabinet_approval = cabinet_approval_decrease }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = JAP_cabinet_approval
			min = 1
			max = 100
		}
		set_variable = { JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance_var = cabinet_approval_decrease }
		multiply_variable = { JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance_var = -0.1 }
		JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance = yes
		set_temp_variable = { opposition_members = 100 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { opposition_members = JAP_cabinet_approval }
		set_variable = { JAP_cabinet_disapproval = opposition_members }
	}

	JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_government = fascism }
			add_popularity = { ideology = fascism popularity = JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance_var }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_government = monarchism }
			add_popularity = { ideology = monarchism popularity = JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance_var }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_government = nationalist }
			add_popularity = { ideology = nationalist popularity = JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance_varr }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_government = conservative }
			add_popularity = { ideology = conservative popularity = JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance_var }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_government = Liberal_Conservatism }
			add_popularity = { ideology = Liberal_Conservatism popularity = JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance_var }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_government = centrist }
			add_popularity = { ideology = centrist popularity = JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance_var }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_government = liberal }
			add_popularity = { ideology = liberal popularity = JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance_var }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_government = democratic }
			add_popularity = { ideology = democratic popularity = JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance_var }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_government = Social_Democratic }
			add_popularity = { ideology = Social_Democratic popularity = JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance_var }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_government = socialist }
			add_popularity = { ideology = socialist popularity = JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance_var }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_government = neutrality }
			add_popularity = { ideology = neutrality popularity = JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance_var }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_government = communism }
			add_popularity = { ideology = communism popularity = JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance_var }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_government = trotskyism }
			add_popularity = { ideology = trotskyism popularity = JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance_var }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_government = maoism }
			add_popularity = { ideology = maoism popularity = JAP_Party_Popularity_Balance_var }
		}
	}

	JAP_Expanded_Housing_Increase_Effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = JAP_Expanded_Housing_1
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = JAP_Expanded_Housing_1
				add_idea = JAP_Expanded_Housing_2
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_ideas = JAP_Expanded_Housing_1
		}
	}

				index = 0
			}

				array = JAP_line_graph_30_graph_offsets
				index = 0
			}
			remove_from_array = {
				array = JAP_line_graph_30_graph_slopes
				index = 0
			}
			remove_from_array = {
				array = JAP_line_graph_30_numbers
				index = 0
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { JAP_line_graph_30_offset_temp = JAP_line_graph_30_last_y_position }
		multiply_temp_variable = { JAP_line_graph_30_offset_temp = -1 }
		add_to_array = { JAP_line_graph_30_graph_offsets = JAP_line_graph_30_offset_temp }
		set_temp_variable = { JAP_line_graph_30_difference = JAP_line_graph_30_y_position }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { JAP_line_graph_30_difference = JAP_line_graph_30_last_y_position }
		divide_temp_variable = { JAP_line_graph_30_difference = 2 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { JAP_line_graph_30_difference = 50 }
		add_to_array = { JAP_line_graph_30_graph_slopes = JAP_line_graph_30_difference }
		add_to_array = { JAP_line_graph_30_numbers = JAP_line_graph_30_count }
	}
	JAP_monthly_refresh = {
		add_to_array = { JAP_line_graph_months_array = JAP_lg_months_var }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_line_graph_months_array^num > 17 } }
			remove_from_array = {
				array = JAP_line_graph_months_array
				index = 0
			}
		}
	}
	JAP_1st_every_monthly_refresh = {
		add_to_variable = { month_counter = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { month_counter = 13 } }
			set_variable = { month_counter = 1 }
		}
		JAP = {
			add_to_variable = { JAP_lg_months_var = 1 }
			JAP_monthly_refresh = yes
		}
	}
	JAP_politics_decisions_fire = {
		add_political_power = -50
	}

### National Radicalism #############################################################################
	JAP_Rightist_Unrest_calculation = {
		set_temp_variable = { unrest_factor = JAP_Rightist_Unrest_Level }
		divide_temp_variable = { unrest_factor = 5 }
		set_variable = { JAP_Rightist_Unrest_construction = -0.15 }
		set_variable = { JAP_Rightist_Unrest_stability = -0.2 }
		set_variable = { JAP_Rightist_Unrest_consumers = 0.05 }
		multiply_variable = {
			var = JAP_Rightist_Unrest_construction
			value = unrest_factor
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			var = JAP_Rightist_Unrest_stability
			value = unrest_factor
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			var = JAP_Rightist_Unrest_consumers
			value = unrest_factor
		}

		JAP_Unrest_GUI_Calculations = yes
	}

### Peace deal Mission #############################################################################
	JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_var_set_up = {
		set_variable = { JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_USA_var = 0.4 }
		set_variable = { JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_SOV_var = 0.4 }
		set_variable = { JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_JAP_var = 0.2 }
	}

	JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_var_calculator = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					target = 1
				}
			}
			divide_temp_variable = {
				JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_USA_var_temp = 3
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = {
				JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_USA_var_temp = -1
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_SOV_var = JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_USA_var_temp
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_JAP_var = JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_USA_var_temp
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					target = 2
				}
			}
			divide_temp_variable = {
				JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_SOV_var_temp = 3
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = {
				JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_SOV_var_temp = -1
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_USA_var = JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_SOV_var_temp
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_JAP_var = JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_SOV_var_temp
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					target = 3
				}
			}
			divide_temp_variable = {
				JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_JAP_var_temp = 3
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = {
				JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_JAP_var_temp = -1
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_SOV_var = JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_JAP_var_temp
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_USA_var = JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_JAP_var_temp
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_total_var = 0
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_JAP_var
			min = 0
			max = 1
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = {
			JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_total_var = JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_JAP_var
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_USA_var
			min = 0
			max = 1
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = {
			JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_total_var = JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_USA_var
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_SOV_var
			min = 0
			max = 1
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = {
			JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_total_var = JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_SOV_var
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_total_var > 1
				}
			}
			subtract_from_temp_variable = {
				JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_total_var = 1
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				random_list = {
					10 = {
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							check_variable = {
								JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_USA_var < JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_total_var
							}
						}
						subtract_from_variable = {
							JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_USA_var = JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_total_var
						}
					}
					10 = {
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							check_variable = {
								JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_SOV_var < JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_total_var
							}
						}
						subtract_from_variable = {
							JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_SOV_var = JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_total_var
						}
					}
					10 = {
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							check_variable = {
								JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_JAP_var < JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_total_var
							}
						}
						subtract_from_variable = {
							JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_JAP_var = JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_total_var
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_total_var < 1
				}
			}
			subtract_from_temp_variable = {
				JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_total_var = 1
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = {
				JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_total_var = -1
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				random_list = {
					10 = {
						add_to_variable = {
							JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_USA_var = JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_total_var
						}
					}
					10 = {
						add_to_variable = {
							JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_SOV_var = JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_total_var
						}
					}
					10 = {
						add_to_variable = {
							JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_JAP_var = JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_total_var
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_JAP_var_temp = 0
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_USA_var_temp = 0
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_SOV_var_temp = 0
		}
	}
	Add_JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_USA_var = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = Add_JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_USA_var_tt
		set_temp_variable = {
			target = 1
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_USA_var
			value = JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_support_USA_var_temp
		}
		JAP_Peace_Deal_Mission_var_calculator = yes
	}

### ReMilitarisation Mission #############################################################################
	JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_Calculator = {

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Military_var_temp > 0 } }
			subtract_from_variable = { JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Ministry_var = JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Military_var_temp }
			set_variable = { JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Military_var_temp = 0 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Military_var_temp < 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Ministry_var = JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Military_var_temp }
			set_variable = { JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Military_var_temp = 0 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Ministry_var_temp > 0 } }
			subtract_from_variable = { JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Military_var = JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Ministry_var_temp }
			set_variable = { JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Ministry_var_temp = 0 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Ministry_var_temp < 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Military_var = JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Ministry_var_temp }
			set_variable = { JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Ministry_var_temp = 0 }
		}

		clamp_variable = {
			var = JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Military_var
			min = 0
			max = 1
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Ministry_var
			min = 0
			max = 1
		}

		JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_Dynamic_Modifier_Calculator = yes
	}

	JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_Dynamic_Modifier_Calculator = {
		set_variable = { JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Military_var_modifier = JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Military_var }
		set_variable = { JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Ministry_var_modifier = JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Ministry_var }
		multiply_variable = { JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Military_var_modifier = 0.2 }
		multiply_variable = { JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Ministry_var_modifier = 0.2 }
		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	}

	Add_JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Military_var = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = Add_JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Military_var_tt
		add_to_variable = {
			var = JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Military_var
			value = JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Military_var_temp
		}
		JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_Calculator = yes
	}
	Add_JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Ministry_var = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = Add_JAP_ReMilitarisation_Mission_support_Ministry_var_tt
		add_to_variable = {

### Shakaito Split #############################################################################
	JAP_Shakaito_Split_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = JAP_Shakaito_Split_tt_1
		custom_effect_tooltip = JAP_Shakaito_Split_tt_2
		clr_country_flag = JAP_Unification_of_SDP
		set_variable = { shakaito_party_popularity_var = party_popularity_100@Social_Democratic }
		add_to_variable = { shakaito_party_popularity_var = party_popularity_100@socialist }
		divide_variable = { shakaito_party_popularity_var = 2 }
		clamp_variable = { var = shakaito_party_popularity_var min = 0 max = 100 }
		set_popularities = {
			Liberal_Conservatism = party_popularity_100@Liberal_Conservatism
			Social_Democratic = shakaito_party_popularity_var
			socialist = shakaito_party_popularity_var
			centrist = party_popularity_100@centrist
			communism = party_popularity_100@communism
			monarchism = party_popularity_100@monarchism
			fascism = party_popularity_100@fascism
			nationalist = party_popularity_100@nationalist
			conservative =party_popularity_100@conservative
			neutrality = party_popularity_100@neutrality
			trotskyism = party_popularity_100@trotskyism
			maoism = party_popularity_100@maoism
			Christian_Democratic = party_popularity_100@Christian_Democratic
			Social_Liberalism = party_popularity_100@Social_Liberalism
			democratic = party_popularity_100@democratic
			liberal = party_popularity_100@liberal
			islamism = party_popularity_100@islamism
			radical_shiaism = party_popularity_100@radical_shiaism
			Salafism = party_popularity_100@Salafism
		}
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = Social_Democratic
			long_name = JAP_Shakaito_Uha_party_long
			name = JAP_Shakaito_Uha_party
		}
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = socialist
			long_name = JAP_Shakaito_Saha_party_long
			name = JAP_Shakaito_Saha_party
		}
		JAP_LHC_election_result_effect = yes
	}

	JAP_Shakaito_Reunification_effect = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = JAP_Shakaito_Formation_tt
		set_country_flag = JAP_Unification_of_SDP
		set_variable = { shakaito_party_popularity_var = party_popularity@Social_Democratic }
		add_to_variable = { shakaito_party_popularity_var = party_popularity@socialist }
		clamp_variable = { var = shakaito_party_popularity_var min = 0 max = 1 }

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { party_popularity@Social_Democratic > party_popularity@socialist } }
			set_political_party = {ideology = Socialist popularity = 0.01 }
			set_political_party = {ideology = Social_Democratic popularity = 0.01 }
			add_popularity = { ideology = Social_Democratic popularity = shakaito_party_popularity_var }
		}
		else = {
			set_political_party = {ideology = Social_Democratic popularity = 0.01 }
			set_political_party = {ideology = Socialist popularity = 0.01 }
			add_popularity = { ideology = Socialist popularity = shakaito_party_popularity_var }
		}
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = Social_Democratic
			long_name = JAP_Shakaito_party_long
			name = JAP_Shakaito_party
		}
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = socialist
			long_name = JAP_Shakaito_party_long
			name = JAP_Shakaito_party
		}
		JAP_LHC_election_result_effect = yes
	}

### Formation of LDP #############################################################################
	JAP_Formation_of_LDP = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = JAP_LDP_Formation_tt
		set_country_flag = JAP_Formation_of_LDP
		set_variable = { jiminto_party_popularity_var = party_popularity@Liberal_Conservatism }
		add_to_variable = { jiminto_party_popularity_var = party_popularity@conservative }
		clamp_variable = { var = jiminto_party_popularity_var min = 0 max = 1 }

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { party_popularity@Liberal_Conservatism > party_popularity@conservative } }
			set_political_party = { ideology = conservative popularity = 0.01 }
			set_political_party = { ideology = Liberal_Conservatism popularity = 0.01 }
			add_popularity = { ideology = Liberal_Conservatism popularity = jiminto_party_popularity_var }
		}
		else = {
			set_political_party = { ideology = Liberal_Conservatism popularity = 0.01 }
			set_political_party = { ideology = conservative popularity = 0.01 }
			add_popularity = { ideology = conservative popularity = jiminto_party_popularity_var }
		}

		set_party_name = {
			ideology = conservative
			long_name = JAP_LDP_party_long
			name = JAP_LDP_party
		}
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = Liberal_Conservatism
			long_name = JAP_LDP_party_long
			name = JAP_LDP_party
		}

		JAP_LHC_election_result_effect = yes
	}

	JAP_LDP_Split_effect = {
		clr_country_flag = JAP_Formation_of_LDP
		set_variable = { jiminto_party_popularity_var = party_popularity_100@Liberal_Conservatism }
		add_to_variable = { jiminto_party_popularity_var = party_popularity_100@conservative }
		divide_variable = { jiminto_party_popularity_var = 2 }
		clamp_variable = { var = jiminto_party_popularity_var min = 0 max = 100 }

		set_political_party = {ideology = conservative popularity = jiminto_party_popularity_var }
		set_political_party = {ideology = Liberal_Conservatism popularity = jiminto_party_popularity_var }
		set_popularities = {
			Liberal_Conservatism = jiminto_party_popularity_var
			Social_Democratic = party_popularity_100@Social_Democratic
			socialist = party_popularity_100@socialist
			centrist = party_popularity_100@centrist
			communism = party_popularity_100@communism
			monarchism = party_popularity_100@monarchism
			fascism = party_popularity_100@fascism
			nationalist = party_popularity_100@nationalist
			conservative = jiminto_party_popularity_var
			neutrality = party_popularity_100@neutrality
			trotskyism = party_popularity_100@trotskyism
			maoism = party_popularity_100@maoism
			Christian_Democratic = party_popularity_100@Christian_Democratic
			Social_Liberalism = party_popularity_100@Social_Liberalism
			democratic = party_popularity_100@democratic
			liberal = party_popularity_100@liberal
			islamism = party_popularity_100@islamism
			radical_shiaism = party_popularity_100@radical_shiaism
			Salafism = party_popularity_100@Salafism
		}

		set_party_name = {
			ideology = Liberal_Conservatism
			long_name = JAP_Jiyuto_party_long
			name = JAP_Jiyuto_party_party
		}
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = conservative
			long_name = JAP_Minshuto_party_long
			name = JAP_Minshuto_party
		}

		JAP_LHC_election_result_effect = yes
	}
